  <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: left">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><i>Operating Expenses </i></p>
  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">Comparing the three months ended March 31, 2025, to the three months ended March 31, 2026, total operating expenses decreased&#160;by&#160;5.2% from&#160;$5.8&#160;million to&#160;$5.5&#160;million. As a percentage of total revenue, total operating expenses increased&#160;5.1 percentage points to 67.9%. The dollar value decrease in operating expenses was due to decreases in the compensation and benefits, general and administrative, fund distribution and other, and sub-advisory fees categories, partly offset by an&#160;increase&#160;in depreciation expense.</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">Comparing the six months ended March 31, 2025, to the six&#160;months ended March 31, 2026, total operating expenses&#160;decreased by&#160;4.0%&#160;from&#160;$11.7&#160;million to&#160;$11.2 million. As a percentage of total revenue, total operating expenses&#160;increased&#160;6.6 percentage points to&#160;68.3%. The dollar value decrease in operating expenses was due to decreases in the compensation and benefits, fund distribution and other, and sub-advisory fees categories, partly offset by increases in general and administrative and&#160;depreciation expense categories.</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;"><span style="text-decoration: underline; ">Compensation and Benefits Expense</span>: Comparing the three months ended March 31, 2025, to the three months ended March 31, 2026, compensation and benefits expense&#160;decreased&#160;by&#160;4.2%, from&#160;$2.7&#160;million to&#160;$2.6 million. As a percentage of total revenue, compensation and benefits expense&#160;increased&#160;2.7 percentage points to 31.5%.&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">Comparing the six months ended March 31, 2025, to the six months ended March 31, 2026, compensation and benefits expense&#160;decreased by&#160;7.4%, from&#160;$5.4 million to&#160;$5.0&#160;million. As a percentage of total revenue, compensation and benefits expense&#160;increased 1.9&#160;percentage points to 30.6%.</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">In each period, the dollar value decrease in compensation and benefit expense was due to a&#160;decrease in incentive-based compensation in the current period.&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;"><span style="text-decoration: underline; ">General and Administrative Expense</span>: Comparing the three months ended March 31, 2025, to the three months ended March 31, 2026, general and administrative expense&#160;decreased&#160;by 9.0%, from&#160;$1.8 million to&#160;$1.6 million. As a percentage of total revenue, general and administrative expense&#160;increased&#160;0.6 percentage points to 20.1%. The dollar value decrease in general and administrative expense was primarily due to decreased sales and distribution expense&#160;in the current period.&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">Comparing the six months ended March 31, 2025, to the six months ended March 31, 2026, general and administrative expense&#160;increased&#160;by 2.9%, from&#160;$3.5&#160;million to&#160;$3.6&#160;million. As a percentage of total revenue, general and administrative expense&#160;increased&#160;3.4 percentage points to 21.6%. The increase in general and administrative expense was primarily due to the termination on January 1, 2026, of the definitive agreement signed with STF Management, LP in March 2025 and the related expense of previously capitalized costs&#160;in the current period.&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;"><span style="text-decoration: underline; ">Fund Distribution and Other Expense</span>: Fund distribution and other expense consists primarily of financial institution fees incurred by us for distribution of the Hennessy Funds and also for the operations of the Hennessy Sustainable ETF. Fund distribution and other expense does not include sub&#8209;advisory fees, which are shown separately.</p>
  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">The distribution component of fund distribution and other expense consists of fees paid to various financial institutions that offer the Hennessy Funds as potential investments to their clients. When the Hennessy Funds are purchased through one of these financial institutions, the institution typically charges an asset&#8209;based fee, which is recorded as a fund distribution expense on our statement of operations&#160;to the extent paid by us. The Hennessy Mutual Funds, but not the Hennessy Sustainable ETF, may be purchased directly and when purchased directly, we do not incur any such expense. These fees generally increase or decrease in line with the net assets of the Hennessy Funds held through these financial institutions, which are affected by inflows, outflows, and fund performance. In addition, some financial institutions charge a minimum fee if the average daily net assets of a Hennessy Fund held by such an institution are less than a threshold amount. In such cases, we pay the minimum fee.</p>
  <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: left">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">The distribution component of fund distribution and other expenses is affected by many factors, including the following:</p>
  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

     <td style="width: auto;"> <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif;font-size: 10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">average daily net assets held by financial institutions;</p> </td>

  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">The other component of fund distribution and other expense consists of fees incurred by us for the operations of the Hennessy Sustainable ETF. We receive a unitary investment advisory fee from the Hennessy Sustainable ETF and then pay all of its operating expenses (with limited exceptions), including fund administration, fund accounting, transfer agency, custody, licensing, audit, and tax services.</p>
  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">Comparing the three months ended March 31, 2025, to the three months ended March 31, 2026, fund distribution and other expense&#160;decreased by 7.2%, from&#160;$0.3 million to&#160;$0.2 million. As a percentage of total revenue, fund distribution and other expense&#160;increased&#160;0.2 percentage points to&#160;2.9%.</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">Comparing the six months ended March 31, 2025, to the six&#160;months ended March 31, 2026, fund distribution and other expense&#160;decreased&#160;by 12.8%, from&#160;$0.52&#160;million to&#160;$0.45&#160;million. As a percentage of total revenue, fund distribution and other expense&#160;increased&#160;0.1&#160;percentage points to&#160;2.8%.</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">In each period,&#160;the dollar value decrease in fund distribution and other expense&#160;was primarily due&#160;to decreased average daily net assets across the Hennessy Funds in the current period, which in turn decreases the fees we pay to financial institutions.&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;"><span style="text-decoration: underline; ">Sub-Advisory Fees Expense</span>: Comparing the three months ended March 31, 2025, to the three months ended March 31, 2026, sub-advisory fees expense&#160;decreased by 2.7%, from&#160;$1.03&#160;million to&#160;$1.00 million. As a percentage of total revenue, sub-advisory fees expense&#160;increased&#160;1.2 percentage points to 12.3%.&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">Comparing the six months ended March 31, 2025, to the six months ended March 31, 2026, sub-advisory fees expense&#160;decreased by 5.4%, from&#160;$2.2&#160;million to&#160;$2.0 million. As a percentage of total revenue, sub-advisory fees expense&#160;increased&#160;1.0 percentage points to 12.4%.</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">In each period,&#160;the dollar value decrease in sub-advisory fees expense&#160;was due to decreased average daily net assets of the sub&#8209;advised Hennessy Funds in the current period.&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;"><span style="text-decoration: underline; ">Depreciation Expense</span>: Comparing the three months ended March 31, 2025, to the three months ended March 31, 2026, depreciation expense&#160;increased&#160;by&#160;24.6%&#160;from&#160;$0.07 million to $0.09&#160;million. As a percentage of total revenue, depreciation expense&#160;increased&#160;0.4&#160;percentage points to&#160;1.1%.&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">Comparing the&#160;six months ended March 31, 2025, to the six months ended March 31, 2026, depreciation expense&#160;increased by&#160;16.9%&#160;from&#160;$0.13 million to&#160;$0.15 million. As a percentage of total revenue, depreciation expense&#160;increased&#160;0.2 percentage points to&#160;0.9%.</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">In each period, the increase in depreciation expense&#160;resulted from more fixed asset purchases in the current period.</p>
  <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: left">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><i>Interest Income </i></p>
  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">Interest income consists of interest earned on cash and cash equivalents. Comparing the three months ended March 31, 2025, to the three months ended March 31, 2026, interest income&#160;decreased from&#160;$0.7 million to&#160;$0.6 million.&#160;Comparing the six months ended March 31, 2025, to the six months ended March 31, 2026, interest income&#160;decreased&#160;from&#160;$1.4&#160;million to&#160;$1.3&#160;million. In each period, the decrease in interest income resulted from decreased interest rates in the current period.</p>
  <p style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt; text-indent: 36pt;">&#160;</p>

  <p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: left">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><i>Interest Expense</i></p>
  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">Comparing the three months ended March 31, 2025, to the three months ended March 31, 2026, interest expense&#160;increased from&#160;$0.57 million to&#160;$0.58 million.&#160;Comparing the six months ended March 31, 2025, to the six months ended March 31, 2026, interest expense&#160;increased&#160;from&#160;$1.14&#160;million to&#160;$1.15&#160;million. In each period, the increase in interest expense&#160;was due to the manner in which interest expense is calculated under U.S. GAAP. The issuance costs related to the 2026 Notes that have been capitalized are amortized over time and therefore increase the carrying amount of the 2026 Notes. As the carrying amount of the 2026 Notes increases, the interest expense on the 2026 Notes for financial statement purposes also increases.</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><i>Income Tax Expense </i></p>
  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">Comparing the three months ended March 31, 2025, to the three months ended March 31, 2026, income tax expense decreased&#160;by&#160;24.4%, from&#160;$1.0&#160;million to $0.7&#160;million.&#160;Comparing the six months ended March 31, 2025, to the six months ended March 31, 2026, income tax expense&#160;decreased by&#160;29.5%, from&#160;$2.1&#160;million to $1.5&#160;million. In each period, the decrease in income tax expense was due to decreased net operating income.</p>
  <p style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; margin: 0pt; text-indent: 36pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><i>Net Income </i></p>
  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">Comparing the three months ended March 31, 2025, to the three months ended March 31, 2026, net income&#160;decreased by 26.1%, from $2.6 million to&#160;$1.9 million.&#160;Comparing the six months ended March 31, 2025, to the six months ended March 31, 2026, net income&#160;decreased&#160;by 29.1%, from $5.4&#160;million to&#160;$3.8&#160;million. In each period, the decrease in net income was primarily due to decreased revenue in the current period.</p>
  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b>Critical Accounting Policies and Estimates</b></p>
  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">Our financial statements and accompanying notes are prepared in accordance with U.S. GAAP, which require the use of estimates, judgments, and assumptions that affect the reported amounts of assets and liabilities at the date of the financial statements and the reported amounts of revenues and expenses during the periods presented. These accounting policies, methods, and estimates are an integral part of the financial statements prepared by management and are based upon management&#8217;s current judgments. Those judgments are normally based on knowledge and experience with regard to past and current events and assumptions about future events. Certain accounting policies, methods, and estimates are particularly sensitive because of their significance to the financial statements and because future events affecting them may differ markedly from management&#8217;s current judgment. For a discussion of the accounting policies and estimates that we believe are most critical to understanding our results of operations and financial position, see the section titled &#8220;Management&#8217;s Discussion and Analysis of Financial Condition and Results of Operations&#8221; in our Annual Report on Form&#160;10&#8209;K for the fiscal year ended September 30, 2025. There has been no material change to our critical accounting estimates disclosed in such Annual Report.</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">&#160;</p>

  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b>Evaluation of Disclosure Controls and Procedures</b></p>
  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">Management performed an evaluation of the effectiveness of the design and operation of the Company&#8217;s disclosure controls and procedures, as such term is defined in Rules 13a-15(e) and 15d-15(e) under the Securities Exchange Act of 1934, as amended (the &#8220;Exchange Act&#8221;), as of the end of the period covered by this report. Based on such evaluation, management, including the Company&#8217;s principal executive officer and principal financial officer, concluded that the Company&#8217;s disclosure controls and procedures are effective as of the end of the period covered by this report.</p>
  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b>Changes in Internal Control over Financial Reporting</b></p>
  <p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
  <p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;text-indent:36pt;">There have been no changes in the Company&#8217;s internal control over financial reporting as defined in Rules&#160;13a-15(f) of the Exchange Act that occurred during the fiscal quarter ended March 31, 2026, and that have materially affected, or are reasonably likely to materially affect, our internal control over financial reporting.</p>
